Every failed promoter run AND every failed delta-sweep run carries detail.error verbatim \"The usage limit has been reached\". That string appears NOWHERE in the repo (grep -rn over packages/apps/libs = 0 hits), so it is an upstream refusal, not our code.\n2. model_id IS NULL on all 24 promoter failures — the refusal lands before a model is selected, i.e. at account/dispatch level.\n3. The dispatch path is codex: work-items-admission-promoter.ts:1943 resolves `opts.model?.trim() || LEARNING_MODEL_SPEC`, and LEARNING_MODEL_SPEC = 'gpt-5.6-sol:xhigh' (learning/model-policy.ts:21-23).\n4. NO per-routine override exists: payload_template->>'model' IS NULL for all 12 %admission% routines, so the constant is what actually runs.\n5. accounts:probe-capacity { provider:'codex' } at 16:2xZ returned probed:7, unwalled:[], serving:[], confirmedWalled:[chatgpt-sub-cli, aviynw2_codex, aviynw_3, aviynw4_codex, aviynw5_codex, aviynw3_codex, aviynw6_codex] — all 7 status 429, utilization7d 1.0. This is the live upstream reading, not the accounts:status projection.\n\nBLAST RADIUS: exactly the LLM-backed admission routines — promoter + delta-sweep (both failing on the same string). work-item-admission-fail-open is a SEPARATE deterministic runner that makes no model call, still firing (16:18) and admitting rows as admission='unreviewed'. That is why queue distribution is NOT starved, as the item's own text says.\n\nSCOPE LIMIT: this probe measured the DEFAULT (sol/generic) codex bucket, which is the one the promoter uses. It says nothing about the 'gpt-reserve' (luna) bucket.\n\nNOT ACTIONABLE AS AN ENGINEERING FIX: the item's stated ask, \"Restore the durable work-item-admission-promoter routine\", has no code repair — the routine is active (last_fired_at 16:02, next_fire_at 16:30) and correct.
